[32m+[m[32m//! Keeping the chart layers and what the panes do with them in step.[m
[32m+[m[32m//![m
[32m+[m[32m//! The layer actions a pane's menu could not apply itself, the footprint[m
[32m+[m[32m//! change, the mask the panes read, and the restore that puts the saved layer[m
[32m+[m[32m//! states back. Named `chart_layers_wiring` rather than `chart_layers`[m
[32m+[m[32m//! because [`crate::chart_layers`] is the layer model itself: this is the[m
[32m+[m[32m//! window's side of the wire, and one name for both would make the import[m
[32m+[m[32m//! at the top of `app.rs` ambiguous to a reader.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse std::time::Instant;[m
[32m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36muse crate::chart_layers::{self, ChartLayer};[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse super::QuantickApp;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mimpl QuantickApp {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// What a pane's layer menu could not switch itself.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Drained right after the canvas, so the frame that clicked the entry is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the frame that applies it. Both wishes reach the real owner — the shared[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// style, the indicator state file — instead of a second copy on the pane.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn apply_layer_actions(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let actions = std::mem::take(&mut self.layer_actions);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(visible) = actions.grid {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.style.canvas.grid_enabled = visible;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The appearance panel's own edits bump this; the renderer and the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // style log both read it to know something moved.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.style_revision = self.style_revision.saturating_add(1);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if actions.indicators_changed {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.mark_indicator_state_dirty();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if actions.footprint_changed {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            crate::footprint_config::save([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.workspace.footprint_settings_path(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                &self.footprint_config,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if actions.open_footprint_settings {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.surfaces.footprint_settings.open();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Settle every tab's paper panel and hand its acknowledgement to the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// window's one toast.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// # One lane, and what that cost[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The panel used to draw its own toast: the same `CENTER_BOTTOM` anchor[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// as `ToastSurface`, 96px up instead of 44, on a 4-second clock instead[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// of 8. Two acknowledgements could therefore sit in one lane, at two[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// heights, disagreeing about how long an acknowledgement lasts. There is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// one now, and this is where the panel's messages join it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// # Every tab, not just the one on screen[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// `settle` runs for all of them because the jobs it finishes — an[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// export, an import — belong to the tab that started them, and a trader[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// who starts an export and then looks at another chart should not have[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// to come back for it to land. The acknowledgements follow: a stop[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// filling on a chart the trader is not looking at is precisely the news[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// they most need, and dropping it silently is what the old per-tab toast[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// did.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// A message from a background tab is **named**, because an unlabelled[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// "SIM: dropped at the fill" would read as being about the chart on[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// screen.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// # Which message wins a slot that holds one[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The watched tab's, always: it carries no prefix and is posted last, so[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// it takes the slot from any background message raised on the same[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// frame. Among background tabs the **first** in tab order wins and the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// rest of that frame are dropped — the same first-wins rule[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// `SurfaceResponse::merge` uses for a request that carries a value, so[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the window has one tie-break rule rather than two. Posting each of[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// them in turn would look like it showed them all and would in fact[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// show whichever `tabs.iter()` reached last, which is tab order deciding[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// in silence.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn settle_paper_panels(&mut self, now: Instant) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Self {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tabs,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            active_tab,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            surfaces,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            ..[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        } = self;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let mut watched = None;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let mut background = None;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for (index, tab) in tabs.iter_mut().enumerate() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tab.paper.settle();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let Some(message) = tab.paper.take_toast() else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                continue;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if index == *active_tab {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                watched = Some(message);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            } else if background.is_none() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // The interpunct is the window's own separator — the status[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // bar, the tape's axis caption and the layout strip all use[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // it, and the messages themselves already carry a colon[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // (`SIM: …`). A second one would read as two labels.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                background = Some(format!("{} · {message}", tab.symbol));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(message) = background {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            surfaces.toast.note(message, now);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(message) = watched {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            surfaces.toast.note(message, now);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Apply what the footprint settings window settled on.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Whatever is edited also becomes the window default, which is what a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// trader configuring their first chart means; a second chart diverges[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// only when they configure it too.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn apply_footprint_change(&mut self, change: crate::surfaces::FootprintChange) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let side = self.active_tab().focused_side();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        match change {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            crate::surfaces::FootprintChange::Applied(edited) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.active_tab_mut().pane_mut(side).footprint_override = Some((*edited).clone());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.footprint_config = *edited;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                crate::footprint_config::save([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    self.workspace.footprint_settings_path(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    &self.footprint_config,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            crate::surfaces::FootprintChange::ResetToDefault => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.active_tab_mut().pane_mut(side).footprint_override = None;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The visibility this app persists, as one bit per layer.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Read off the active tab's flow pane: the file records the canvas[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// quantick is built around, the same scope the indicator state file has[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// (see [`Self::maintain_indicator_state`]). A tab's second pane opens[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// matching it and is in-session from there.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn layer_mask(&self) -> u32 {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.active_tab().flow_pane.layer_mask(&self.style)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Save the layer visibility when it differs from what is on disk.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Called once per frame instead of from each switch: the layers are owned[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// by four different pieces of chrome, and a save hook on each is four[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// chances to forget one.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn maintain_chart_layers(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let mask = self.layer_mask();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Layer state is per-pane, and this reads the *active* tab's flow pane[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // — so activating a tab whose chart is set up differently changes the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // mask with nobody having touched a switch. Left alone, Ctrl+Tab[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // records the other tab's opinion as the trader's choice, thrashes the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // file between two of them, and fills the log below with switches no[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // hand moved. A different chart answering is a re-baseline, not an[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // edit; the file keeps whatever the last real switch put there.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let tab = self.active_tab().id;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if tab != self.workspace.layers().tab() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.workspace.layers_mut().rebaseline(tab, mask);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if mask == self.workspace.layers().mask() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Name every switch that moved, before writing it down.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        //[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // This file is the trader's own answer, and it outranks the shipped[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // default from the next launch on — so a layer that goes off without a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // click is not a display glitch, it is a choice attributed to someone[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // who never made it, and it lasts. The bug that motivated this line was[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // exactly that shape and cost a day to chase, because the only record[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // was the file itself: a trio of `false`s with no timestamp, no[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // sequence and nothing to say whether a hand had been near them.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        //[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Off the frame path in every sense that matters: the mask compare[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // above already gates it, so this runs on the frames a switch actually[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // moves — a handful in a session — and not one of the other 60 a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // second.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let flipped = mask ^ self.workspace.layers().mask();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for (bit, layer) in ChartLayer::ALL.into_iter().enumerate() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if flipped & (1 << bit) == 0 {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                continue;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                event_code = "CHART_LAYER_SWITCHED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                layer = layer.id(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                on = mask & (1 << bit) != 0,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                action = "persist_switch",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "a chart layer switch moved; recording it as the trader's choice"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        chart_layers::save([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.workspace.chart_layers_path(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            &self.active_tab().flow_pane.layer_states(&self.style),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.layers_mut().record(mask);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Apply the saved layer visibility to the tab the app opened with.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Runs before the autostart env vars so an explicit `QUANTICK_*_AUTOSTART`[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// still wins for the run it was set on: a validation session asks for the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// heatmap on the command line and gets it, whatever the file remembers.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn restore_chart_layers(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let defaults = chart_layers::load(self.workspace.chart_layers_path());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Whatever the file said (including nothing at all) is now on screen;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // only a change from here is worth another write.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if defaults.is_empty() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Only reachable when the *shipped* config failed to parse, since[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // every other path in `load` falls back to it — a build-time[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // mistake, and the one launch where saying nothing would be worst:[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // the chart opens on whatever the code decided and no one is told[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // the product's own answer never arrived.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tracing::error!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                event_code = "CHART_LAYERS_UNAVAILABLE",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                path = %self.workspace.chart_layers_path().display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                action = "keep_code_defaults",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "no layer visibility to apply; the shipped config did not parse"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let (tab, mask) = (self.active_tab().id, self.layer_mask());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.workspace.layers_mut().rebaseline(tab, mask);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(grid) = defaults.get(&ChartLayer::Grid) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.style.canvas.grid_enabled = *grid;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.apply_layer_defaults(&defaults);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (tab, mask) = (self.active_tab().id, self.layer_mask());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.layers_mut().rebaseline(tab, mask);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "CHART_LAYERS_RESTORED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            path = %self.workspace.chart_layers_path().display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // `off`, not `hidden`: the map now always speaks for every layer,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // the shipped-off `backfill_divider` included, so a count over it[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // is no longer "how many the trader switched off". Renamed rather[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // than quietly redefined — a field that keeps its name and changes[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // its meaning misleads every dashboard already reading it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            off = defaults.values().filter(|visible| !**visible).count(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            layers = defaults.len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "chart layer visibility restored"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Put every open pane on the saved visibility, once, at startup.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// A tab opened later does *not* come through here: it inherits the layers[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the active tab is showing at that moment (`inherited_layers` in[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// [`Self::adopt_tab`]), which is the live state rather than the map read[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// off disk during boot. The two policies differ on purpose — see the note[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// there — so a reader arriving here for new-tab behaviour is in the wrong[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// function.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn apply_layer_defaults(&mut self, states: &std::collections::BTreeMap<ChartLayer, bool>) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for tab in &mut self.tabs {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Every pane, by address: a default applied to "the flow pane and[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // the time pane" left the second stacked chart on whatever the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // previous defaults were, so one canvas drew the same layer two[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // ways.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            for pane in tab.panes_mut() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.apply_layer_states(states);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32m//! The indicator manager: what the window does about the slots on its panes.[m
[32m+[m[32m//![m
[32m+[m[32m//! Attaching and detaching, the legends and their folds, the settings dialog[m
[32m+[m[32m//! and its draft, the presets, the script hot-reload poll and the state file[m
[32m+[m[32m//! that remembers it all. They are together because they are one feature seen[m
[32m+[m[32m//! from several surfaces: the legend, the dialog and the control plane's[m
[32m+[m[32m//! `indicator.script.attach` all reach the same slots through the same[m
[32m+[m[32m//! [`crate::indicator_worker`] commands, and a change to what a slot is has[m
[32m+[m[32m//! to be made in one file rather than found in six places in `app.rs`.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse std::time::{Duration, Instant};[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse eframe::egui;[m
[32m+[m[32muse smallvec::SmallVec;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::canvas_layout::MAX_CANVAS_PANES;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36muse crate::indicator_legend;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36muse crate::indicator_panel::{self, SettingsDialog, SettingsOutcome};[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36muse crate::indicator_worker::{IndicatorCommand, IndicatorEvent, IndicatorSource, SlotId};[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36muse crate::indicators::IndicatorView;[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::indicators::state_file::{SavedInput, SavedKind};[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::pane::PaneSide;[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::style::CandlePreset;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse super::{QuantickApp, TabSlot};[m
[32m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m/// How often the hot-reload poll checks script files for changes.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36mconst SCRIPT_RELOAD_POLL_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(1_000);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mimpl QuantickApp {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Put one Quantick Pine script on the focused pane behind a fresh slot.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The one door: the script library's click arrives here, and so does an[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// authorized agent's `indicator.script.attach`. Returns the tab, the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// pane and the slot, which is what a caller needs to detach it again.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    pub(crate) fn attach_script_indicator([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        &mut self,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        name: String,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        text: String,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        by_operator: bool,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ) -> (u64, crate::control::PaneSideDto, SlotId) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let slot = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .focused_pane_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .add_indicator(IndicatorSource::Script {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                name: name.clone(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                text,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let owner = self.target_slot(slot);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let kind = SavedKind::Script { name };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.slot_kinds.push((owner, kind.clone()));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Whose slot this is decides who may take it away again: the annotate[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // tier removes what it attached, never what the trader put there. An[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // operator's overlay stays on the one pane it was attached to and[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // out of the layout; the trader's script is a layout edit and goes[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // onto every pane.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if by_operator {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.operator_slots.insert(owner);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.mirror_add(owner, &kind);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_indicator_edit_at(owner.tab, owner.side);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        (owner.tab, owner.side.into(), slot)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Take one slot **an operator attached** off the chart, through the same[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// removal the indicator legend's own button uses.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// `Err` when the slot is one the trader put there: this tier adds and[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// takes back its own, and never removes work done by hand (plan §2.6).[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// `Ok(false)` when there is no such slot at all.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    pub(crate) fn detach_script_indicator(&mut self, slot: u64) -> Result<bool, ()> {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // A slot number is allocated per pane, so several panes can carry the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // same one: the operator's own is the one to take, and matching on the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // number alone would remove whichever pane happened to be registered[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // first — the trader's, as often as not.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let mut known = false;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let mut target = None;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for (owner, _) in &self.slot_kinds {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if owner.slot.0 != slot {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                continue;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            known = true;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if self.operator_slots.contains(owner) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                target = Some(*owner);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                break;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(target) = target else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // A slot that exists but belongs to the trader is refused; one[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // that exists nowhere simply was not there.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return if known { Err(()) } else { Ok(false) };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.remove_indicator_at(target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.operator_slots.remove(&target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        Ok(true)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;33m+[m[1;33m    /// Open the dialog for whichever indicator a gesture on a pane asked[m
[1;33m+[m[1;33m    /// about: the pane header, a collapsed strip, or an overlay's own line.[m
[1;33m+[m[1;33m    ///[m
[1;33m+[m[1;33m    /// Drained here rather than acted on where it was read, because the dialog[m
[1;33m+[m[1;33m    /// belongs to the window and the gestures are read deep inside a pane's[m
[1;33m+[m[1;33m    /// input pass. Both sides of a split are asked, and each request resolves[m
[1;33m+[m[1;33m    /// against the pane that raised it — the legend's rule (MAJOR-4), applied[m
[1;33m+[m[1;33m    /// to the same problem one layer down.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn open_requested_indicator_settings(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let tab_id = self.active_tab().id;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The harness hook waits for the view it names, exactly as the restored[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // hidden flags do: the indicator is born from the worker's first[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Rebuilt, which is several frames after the window opens.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some((index, tab)) = self.harness.settings_autostart() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The focused pane first, then the flow pane. A split tab can open[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // with the *time* pane focused while every indicator — the ones the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // other autostart hook adds, and the ones the state file restores —[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // lives on the flow pane, and a hook that only asked the focused[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // side then waited for a view that was never coming. Silence is the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // worst failure a validation hook can have: the run captures a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // chart with no dialog on it and nothing says why.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let focused = self.active_tab().focused_side();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let found = [focused, PaneSide::Flow].into_iter().find_map(|side| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.tabs[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .find(|candidate| candidate.id == tab_id)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .map(|candidate| candidate.pane(side))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .and_then(|pane| pane.indicators.all().get(index))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .map(|view| (side, view.slot))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if let Some((side, slot)) = found {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.harness.settings_autostart_opened();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.open_indicator_settings_at(TabSlot {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    tab: tab_id,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    side,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                if let Some(dialog) = self.indicator_settings.as_mut() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    dialog.tab = tab;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(tab) = self.tabs.iter_mut().find(|tab| tab.id == tab_id) else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let requests: SmallVec<[(PaneSide, SlotId); MAX_CANVAS_PANES]> = tab[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .panes_with_sides_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .filter_map(|(pane, side)| pane.take_settings_request().map(|slot| (side, slot)))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .collect();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for (side, slot) in requests {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.open_indicator_settings_at(TabSlot {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    tab: tab_id,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    side,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Fold or unfold one pane's indicator legend.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The one place the state changes. The chevron, the menu entry, the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// hotkey and the harness hook all arrive here with the outcome they want,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// so none of them can leave a pane in a state the others would not have[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// produced — and an operator that is not holding the mouse names this[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// call rather than a click.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn set_legend_collapsed(&mut self, tab_id: u64, side: PaneSide, collapsed: bool) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(tab) = self.tabs.iter_mut().find(|tab| tab.id == tab_id) else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tab.pane_mut(side).legend_collapsed = collapsed;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Whether the focused pane's legend is folded — what the menu label and[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the hotkey read before deciding which way to flip it.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn focused_legend_collapsed(&self) -> bool {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let tab = self.active_tab();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tab.pane(tab.focused_side()).legend_collapsed[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Fold or unfold the legend of the pane the chrome speaks for. The menu[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// entry and the hotkey both act through this, so "which chart did that[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// affect" has one answer: the focused one, the same pane every other[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// chrome control acts on.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn set_focused_legend_collapsed(&mut self, collapsed: bool) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let tab_id = self.active_tab().id;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let side = self.active_tab().focused_side();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.set_legend_collapsed(tab_id, side, collapsed);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Flip a slot's render-side eye, wherever the slot lives. Addressed by[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// [`TabSlot`], never by focus: the legend acts on the pane it is drawn[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// on, and the toolbar path builds its target from focus before calling.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn toggle_indicator_hidden_at(&mut self, target: TabSlot) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(tab) = self.tabs.iter_mut().find(|tab| tab.id == target.tab) else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tab.pane_mut(target.side)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .indicators[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .toggle_hidden(target.slot);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.mirror_hidden(target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_indicator_edit_at(target.tab, target.side);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Remove a slot, wherever it lives. UI first (the entry vanishes this[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// frame), worker second; events already in flight for the slot are[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// dropped on apply.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn remove_indicator_at(&mut self, target: TabSlot) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if !self.tabs.iter().any(|tab| tab.id == target.tab) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The mirrors first, while the slot's layout position can still be[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // read off the bookkeeping.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.mirror_remove(target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(tab) = self.tabs.iter_mut().find(|tab| tab.id == target.tab) else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let pane = tab.pane_mut(target.side);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        pane.indicators.remove(target.slot);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        pane.indicator_worker[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .send(IndicatorCommand::Remove(target.slot));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.slot_kinds.retain(|(owner, _)| *owner != target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.operator_slots.remove(&target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.script_files.retain(|(owner, ..)| *owner != target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.pending_hidden.retain(|owner| *owner != target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.pending_styles.retain(|(owner, _)| *owner != target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_indicator_edit_at(target.tab, target.side);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Open the settings dialog for a slot, wherever it lives.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn open_indicator_settings_at(&mut self, target: TabSlot) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(view) = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .tabs[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .find(|tab| tab.id == target.tab)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .map(|tab| tab.pane(target.side))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .and_then(|pane| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.indicators[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .all()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .find(|view| view.slot == target.slot)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            })[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.indicator_settings = Some(SettingsDialog {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            slot: target.slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            title: view.label().to_owned(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tab: indicator_panel::SettingsTab::default(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            draft: view.input_values.clone(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            committed: view.input_values.clone(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            previewed: false,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            settled: false,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            preset_label: None,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            preset_name_draft: String::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.indicator_settings_target = target;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Draw each visible pane's indicator legend and run what its rows asked[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// for. Actions resolve against the pane the legend was drawn on — the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// legend must never act on the chart beside it (the audit's MAJOR-4[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// trap, avoided by construction).[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn draw_indicator_legends(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let tab_id = self.active_tab().id;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Whether a context chart is on screen — from what the layout holds[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // and whether the column is collapsed, never from one variant. Matched[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // against `TimeAndFlow` alone, this drew no legend at all on the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // stacked charts of `time+time+flow`, and drew one over the flow chart[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // against a stale rect while the column was put away.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let split = self.active_tab().shows_context_charts();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let mut pending: Vec<(PaneSide, indicator_legend::LegendAction)> = Vec::new();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let shown = self.active_tab().context_panes_shown();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let sides: SmallVec<[PaneSide; MAX_CANVAS_PANES]> = self.active_tab().sides().collect();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for side in sides {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Only a context chart on screen draws a legend: the stack may[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // hold a pane the layout no longer shows.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if let PaneSide::Time(slot) = side[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                && (!split || slot >= shown)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                continue;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let pane = self.active_tab().pane(side);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The rect is last frame's, like every anchor the input path[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // reads; a pane not yet drawn has none and draws no legend.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let Some(mut rect) = pane.last_chart_area else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                continue;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The position HUD owns the very corner of the pane it paints on;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // this legend rides just below it there, and nowhere else. That[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // pane is the one holding the HUD anchor — the focused one, not[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // the flow one, so a split with the time pane focused drops these[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // chips on the *time* pane. The order-flow key stacks under this[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // legend and reads the same offset from the same place, so the two[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // cannot drift apart.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            rect.min.y += indicator_legend::hud_offset_px([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.paper_hud_anchor().is_some()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    && self.active_tab().paper.position_summary().is_some(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The slot being live-previewed by the settings dialog, if it[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // lives on this pane: its legend row wears a "preview" chip.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let preview_slot = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .indicator_settings[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .as_ref()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .filter(|dialog| dialog.previewed)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .filter(|_| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    self.indicator_settings_target.tab == tab_id[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        && self.indicator_settings_target.side == side[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                })[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .map(|dialog| dialog.slot);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            for action in indicator_legend::draw([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                ctx,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.id,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                rect,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.indicators.all(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                preview_slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.legend_collapsed,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            ) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pending.push((side, action));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for (side, action) in pending {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let at = |slot| TabSlot {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tab: tab_id,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                side,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            match action {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                indicator_legend::LegendAction::ToggleHidden(slot) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    self.toggle_indicator_hidden_at(at(slot));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                indicator_legend::LegendAction::OpenSettings(slot) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    self.open_indicator_settings_at(at(slot));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                indicator_legend::LegendAction::Remove(slot) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    self.remove_indicator_at(at(slot));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                indicator_legend::LegendAction::SetCollapsed(collapsed) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    self.set_legend_collapsed(tab_id, side, collapsed);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Draw the settings dialog and execute its outcome. Apply goes through[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the worker (construct anew, replace, replay) — the same path every[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// input change takes, UI or not.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn draw_indicator_settings(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The boot hook's deferred half: fire once a slot can actually show[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // a dialog (see `harness::Harness::wants_indicator_settings_dialog`).[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if self.harness.wants_indicator_settings_dialog() && self.indicator_settings.is_none() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let tab_id = self.active_tab().id;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if let Some(slot) = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .active_tab()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .flow_pane[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .indicators[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .all()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .find(|view| !view.input_values.is_empty())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .map(|view| view.slot)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.open_indicator_settings_at(TabSlot {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    tab: tab_id,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    side: PaneSide::Flow,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.harness.indicator_settings_dialog_opened();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let target = self.indicator_settings_target;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The preset shelf for this slot's kind. A slot with no registered[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // kind (the natives autostart hook deliberately registers none) has[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // nowhere to save to, and the dialog hides the picker.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let preset_names: Option<Vec<String>> = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .slot_kinds[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .find(|(owner, _)| *owner == target)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .map(|(_, kind)| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.indicator_presets[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .names_for(kind)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .map(str::to_owned)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .collect()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let outcome = {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let Self {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                indicator_settings,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tabs,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                ..[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            } = self;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let Some(dialog) = indicator_settings.as_mut() else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The tab the dialog was opened on may have been closed under it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let Some(view) = tabs[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .iter_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .find(|tab| tab.id == target.tab)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .map(|tab| tab.pane_mut(target.side))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .and_then(|pane| pane.indicators.view_mut(dialog.slot))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // The indicator was removed under the dialog.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                *indicator_settings = None;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Follow the live label: an applied edit can retitle the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // indicator (`EMA(9)` → `EMA(21)`), and a dialog that stays open[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // must not keep announcing the old one.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            dialog.title = view.label().to_owned();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The descriptor is read while the style layer beside it is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // written, so the two halves are split off the same view here[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // rather than borrowed twice.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let IndicatorView {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                descriptor, style, ..[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            } = view;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            indicator_panel::draw([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                ctx,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                dialog,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                &descriptor.inputs,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                &descriptor.plots,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                style,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                preset_names.as_deref(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            )[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        match outcome {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            SettingsOutcome::Open => {}[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            SettingsOutcome::Close => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.revert_indicator_settings_preview();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.indicator_settings = None;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            SettingsOutcome::Apply => self.apply_indicator_settings_draft(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            SettingsOutcome::Preview => self.preview_indicator_settings_draft(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            SettingsOutcome::LoadPreset(name) => self.load_indicator_preset(name),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            SettingsOutcome::SavePreset(name) => self.save_indicator_preset(&name),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            SettingsOutcome::DeletePreset(name) => self.delete_indicator_preset(&name),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Style is render-side: the chart already shows it and there is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // nothing to preview or commit, so all that is owed is the file.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Deliberately unlike an input edit — it takes no rebuild and no[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // replay, and it follows the legend's eye, which is also instant[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // and also persisted without an Apply.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            SettingsOutcome::StyleChanged => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let target = self.indicator_settings_target;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.mirror_style(target);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.note_indicator_edit_at(target.tab, target.side);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Replace the dialog's draft with a preset (`None` = the declared[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// defaults) and preview it — the same nudge-and-look contract as a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// slider: Apply commits, Discard reverts. A saved value whose type no[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// longer matches its input (the script evolved under the preset) falls[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// back to that input's default rather than being silently coerced.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn load_indicator_preset(&mut self, name: Option<String>) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let target = self.indicator_settings_target;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(specs) = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .tabs[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .find(|tab| tab.id == target.tab)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .map(|tab| tab.pane(target.side))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .and_then(|pane| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.indicators[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .all()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .find(|view| view.slot == target.slot)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            })[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .map(|view| view.descriptor.inputs.clone())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let saved: Option<Vec<Option<quantick_indicators::InputValue>>> = match &name {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            None => Some(Vec::new()),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Some(name) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let Some(kind) = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .slot_kinds[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .find(|(owner, _)| *owner == target)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .map(|(_, kind)| kind)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.indicator_presets[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .get(kind, name)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    // `map`, not `filter_map`: a stored cell that no longer[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    // reads (a source whose name the dialect dropped, say)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    // still has to hold its index. Dropping it from the list[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    // would slide every value after it one input to the left,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    // and same-typed neighbours would take each other's[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    // settings without a word.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .map(|inputs| inputs.iter().map(SavedInput::to_value).collect())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(saved) = saved else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The same binder the worker binds a saved state file with: one rule[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // for what a saved value means, in one place.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let bound = quantick_indicators::bind_by_position(&specs, &saved);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if bound.kept < saved.len() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The preset on screen is not the preset that was saved. Silence[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // here would show a chart that does not match the name above it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tracing::warn!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                event_code = "INDICATOR_PRESET_REBOUND",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                preset = %name.as_deref().unwrap_or(indicator_panel::DEFAULT_PRESET),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                saved = saved.len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                declared = specs.len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                kept = bound.kept,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                action = "bound_by_position",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "some of this preset's values no longer bind; those inputs took their defaults"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let draft = bound.values;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let label = name.unwrap_or_else(|| indicator_panel::DEFAULT_PRESET.to_owned());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(dialog) = self.indicator_settings.as_mut() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            dialog.draft = draft;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            dialog.preset_label = Some(label);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.preview_indicator_settings_draft();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Save the dialog's current draft under `name` for this slot's kind.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Saving is a file write, never a chart change — the draft on screen[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// stays exactly as it was.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn save_indicator_preset(&mut self, name: &str) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let target = self.indicator_settings_target;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(kind) = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .slot_kinds[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .find(|(owner, _)| *owner == target)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .map(|(_, kind)| kind.clone())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(dialog) = self.indicator_settings.as_mut() else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let inputs: Vec<SavedInput> = dialog.draft.iter().map(SavedInput::from_value).collect();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if self.indicator_presets.insert(&kind, name, inputs) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.indicator_presets[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .save(self.workspace.indicator_presets_path());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            dialog.preset_label = Some(name.trim().to_owned());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            dialog.preset_name_draft.clear();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Forget a preset of this slot's kind. The values on screen stay —[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// deleting a name never touches the chart.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn delete_indicator_preset(&mut self, name: &str) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let target = self.indicator_settings_target;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(kind) = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .slot_kinds[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .find(|(owner, _)| *owner == target)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .map(|(_, kind)| kind.clone())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if self.indicator_presets.remove(&kind, name) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.indicator_presets[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .save(self.workspace.indicator_presets_path());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if let Some(dialog) = self.indicator_settings.as_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                && dialog.preset_label.as_deref() == Some(name)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                dialog.preset_label = None;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Send the open dialog's draft to the worker and keep the dialog open[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// (audit M2): tuning is a nudge-and-look loop, and a dialog that dies[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// on every Apply makes each attempt four clicks. The slot is the one[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the dialog was opened on, not whatever has focus now — clicking[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Apply must not retarget the edit.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn apply_indicator_settings_draft(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let target = self.indicator_settings_target;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(dialog) = self.indicator_settings.as_mut() else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        dialog.committed = dialog.draft.clone();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        dialog.previewed = false;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (slot, values) = (dialog.slot, dialog.draft.clone());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(tab) = self.tabs.iter_mut().find(|tab| tab.id == target.tab) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tab.pane_mut(target.side)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .indicator_worker[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .send(IndicatorCommand::SetInputs {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    values: values.clone(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.mirror_inputs(target, &values);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_indicator_edit_at(target.tab, target.side);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Show the draft on the chart without committing it: same worker path[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// as Apply, but the state file is never marked dirty — what survives a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// restart is always the last Apply, never a slider mid-drag. The worker[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// coalesces a burst of these to its own cadence.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn preview_indicator_settings_draft(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let target = self.indicator_settings_target;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(dialog) = self.indicator_settings.as_mut() else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        dialog.previewed = true;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (slot, values) = (dialog.slot, dialog.draft.clone());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(tab) = self.tabs.iter_mut().find(|tab| tab.id == target.tab) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tab.pane_mut(target.side)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .indicator_worker[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .send(IndicatorCommand::SetInputs { slot, values });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Put the last committed values back on the chart. Close's half of the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// preview contract: a dialog dismissed mid-tuning leaves no trace.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn revert_indicator_settings_preview(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let target = self.indicator_settings_target;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(dialog) = self.indicator_settings.as_ref() else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if !dialog.previewed {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (slot, values) = (dialog.slot, dialog.committed.clone());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(tab) = self.tabs.iter_mut().find(|tab| tab.id == target.tab) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tab.pane_mut(target.side)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .indicator_worker[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .send(IndicatorCommand::SetInputs { slot, values });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Load a library script behind a fresh slot. A file that no longer[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// reads or a script that no longer compiles becomes the slot's error —[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// shown with lines and codes, never silently dropped.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Returns the slot it claimed, so a caller that needs to address the new[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// indicator (restoring saved inputs, say) does not have to guess which[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// one it is.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn add_script_indicator(&mut self, index: usize) -> Option<SlotId> {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let entry = self.script_library.entries().get(index)?;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let name = entry.name.clone();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        match self.script_library.read(index) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Some(Ok(text)) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let (_, _, slot) = self.attach_script_indicator(name, text, false);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let owner = self.target_slot(slot);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // Watch the file so a save reloads it. Registered here, with[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // the add, so the two cannot drift apart.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                if let Some((_, mtime)) = self.script_library.file_info(index) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    self.script_files.push((owner, index, mtime));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                Some(slot)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Some(Err(message)) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tracing::warn!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    event_code = "INDICATOR_SCRIPT_UNREADABLE",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    script = %name,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    error = %message,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    action = "error_slot_shown",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "cannot read an indicator script"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // A click that produces nothing at all is the failure this[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // function's own doc comment rules out. The compile half of[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // that promise runs worker-side; the read half never leaves[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // the UI thread, so the error slot is built here, from the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // same two events the worker would have sent.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let pane = self.focused_pane_mut();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // The same kind string the healthy path derives from its[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // source, so an error slot the trader fixes and reloads keeps[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // whatever they had drawn on its pane.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let slot = pane.indicators.allocate_slot(format!("script.{name}"));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.indicators.apply(IndicatorEvent::Rebuilt {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    descriptor: quantick_indicators::IndicatorDescriptor {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        title: name,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        short_title: None,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        overlay: false,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        plots: Vec::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        fills: Vec::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        inputs: Vec::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    columns: Vec::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    bar_paint: Vec::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    rows: 0,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    inputs: Vec::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    stale: None,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.indicators.apply(IndicatorEvent::Error {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    error: quantick_indicators::EvalError {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        bar_index: 0,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        message,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                Some(slot)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            None => None,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn emit_style_changed(&mut self, applied_preset: Option<CandlePreset>) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let candles = &self.style.candles;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let preset = applied_preset[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .or_else(|| CandlePreset::detect(candles))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .map_or("custom", CandlePreset::log_value);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "CANDLE_STYLE_CHANGED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            revision = self.style_revision,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            preset,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            body_mode = ?candles.body_mode,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            fill_opacity = candles.fill_opacity,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            outline_opacity = candles.outline_opacity,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            outline_width_px = candles.outline_width,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            body_width_fraction = candles.body_width_frac,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            wick_mode = ?candles.wick_color_mode,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            wick_width_px = candles.wick_width,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            chart_background_enabled = self.style.canvas.background_enabled,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            chart_grid_enabled = self.style.canvas.grid_enabled,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = "redraw_only",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "candle appearance changed"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Hot reload: about once a second, compare each file-backed script's[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// mtime; a changed file is re-read and sent as a Reload — recompiled[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// and replayed on success, or flagged stale (the last good version[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// keeps running) on errors. The mtime updates even when the compile[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// fails, so a broken save does not re-fire every second.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn poll_script_files(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if self.script_files.is_empty()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            || self.last_script_poll.elapsed() < SCRIPT_RELOAD_POLL_INTERVAL[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.last_script_poll = Instant::now();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let mut reloads: Vec<(TabSlot, String, String)> = Vec::new();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for (owner, index, seen_mtime) in &mut self.script_files {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let Some((path, mtime)) = self.script_library.file_info(*index) else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                continue;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if mtime == *seen_mtime {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                continue;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            *seen_mtime = mtime;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                Ok(text) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    let name = path[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        .file_name()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        .map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        .unwrap_or_else(|| path.display().to_string());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    reloads.push((*owner, name, text));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                Err(error) => tracing::warn!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    event_code = "INDICATOR_SCRIPT_UNREADABLE",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    script = %path.display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    error = %error,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    action = "reload_skipped",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "cannot re-read a changed indicator script"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                ),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for (owner, name, text) in reloads {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                event_code = "INDICATOR_SCRIPT_RELOAD",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                script = %name,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tab = owner.tab,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane = ?owner.side,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                action = "recompile_and_replay",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "indicator script changed on disk"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // To the worker that owns the slot: the same script loaded on two[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // panes is two slots, and a Reload sent to the wrong one addresses[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // whatever indicator happens to share its number there.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if let Some(tab) = self.tabs.iter_mut().find(|tab| tab.id == owner.tab) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tab.pane_mut(owner.side)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .indicator_worker[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .send(IndicatorCommand::Reload {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        slot: owner.slot,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        source: IndicatorSource::Script { name, text },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Add one of the built-in indicators to the focused pane and register how[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// it restores.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The kind travels to the worker as-is: an id no build ships becomes an[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// error slot naming it, which is why there is no fallback arm here. The[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// one this replaced turned every unrecognised kind into an EMA, so a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// mistyped id put an indicator on the chart that nobody had asked for.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn add_native_indicator(&mut self, id: &str) -> SlotId {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let kind = SavedKind::native(id);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let source = IndicatorSource::Native {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            id: id.to_owned(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            values: Vec::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let slot = self.focused_pane_mut().add_indicator(source);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let owner = self.target_slot(slot);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.slot_kinds.push((owner, kind.clone()));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Every other pane of every tab gets the same indicator now; the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // settled reconciliation binds the layout's copy of it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.mirror_add(owner, &kind);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_indicator_edit_at(owner.tab, owner.side);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        slot[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// An indicator edit happened on the focused pane. Edits that know their[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// pane call [`Self::note_indicator_edit_at`] directly.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn mark_indicator_state_dirty(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (tab, side) = {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let tab = self.active_tab();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            (tab.id, tab.focused_side())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_indicator_edit_at(tab, side);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Undo the dirty mark an add just set — for indicators an env var asked[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// for rather than the user. The slot still exists and still works; it[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// simply does not enter the persisted set.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn forget_last_indicator_state_change(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.slot_kinds.pop();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Apply layout-placed hide flags and styles once their views exist.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The layout itself is written by the edit that changed it — see[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// `layout_wiring` — so nothing here reads a view back.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn maintain_indicator_state(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.apply_pending_indicator_state();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
