[32m+[m[32m//! Writing the cockpit down: what a saved workspace is made of.[m
[32m+[m[32m//![m
[32m+[m[32m//! The write half of the round trip whose read half is[m
[32m+[m[32m//! [`super::workspace_restore`]. `capture_workspace` reads the arrangement[m
[32m+[m[32m//! off the live window, the named-workspace methods put it on disk under a[m
[32m+[m[32m//! name and take it back off again, the `write_*` methods edit one section of[m
[32m+[m[32m//! one cockpit store in place, and the export/import pair moves a whole[m
[32m+[m[32m//! cockpit between machines. They are together because they share the[m
[32m+[m[32m//! stores, the pickers and the one acknowledgement lane -- `note_workspace`[m
[32m+[m[32m//! -- and because nothing outside the window calls any of them.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse std::time::Instant;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse eframe::egui;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::drawings;[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::indicator_worker::{IndicatorCommand, SlotId};[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::indicators::preset_file;[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::state::BarSpec;[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::symbols_file;[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::tab::{CanvasLayout, LegendFold};[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::timezone::TzOffset;[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::ui_state;[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::window_scale;[m
[32m+[m[32muse crate::workspace_store::WorkspacePick;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse quantick_feed::history_reach;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muse super::{QuantickApp, saved_context_intervals};[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mimpl QuantickApp {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The window as it stands, in the form the workspace file records.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Read off the live state rather than accumulated as it changes: the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// arrangement is a dozen fields spread over the tabs and the chrome, and[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// a second copy maintained by every control that moves one of them would[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// be a dozen chances to forget. Saving is rare and event-driven, so[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// reading them all at once costs nothing anyone can see.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn capture_workspace(&self) -> ui_state::Workspace {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (tabs, chrome) = self.capture_arrangement();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        ui_state::Workspace::new([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.workspace.session().save_on_exit(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.window_size,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.active_tab,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tabs,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Some(chrome),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        )[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Every write rewrites the whole file, so the bookmarks have to ride[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // along or saving the startup screen would silently delete them.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        .with_saved(self.workspace.session().bookmarks().to_vec())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // And the recent workspace files, for the same reason: a save that[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // dropped them would empty the Open-recent menu every time the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // trader saved their layout.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        .with_recent(self.workspace.session().recent().to_vec())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // And so does the replay folder, for exactly the same reason: a save[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // that dropped it would send the browser back to nowhere on the next[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // launch, which is the failure this field was added to end. The[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // trader's *pick*, never the folder in use — a run under[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // `QUANTICK_REPLAY_DIR` must not write a QA scratch path into their[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // workspace, and accepting the default home is not a choice either.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        .with_replay_folder(self.replay_view.stored_pick().map(str::to_owned))[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        .with_replay_day_before(self.replay_view.stored_day_before())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // And the starred tools, for the third time and the same reason. They[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // are already on disk the moment the star is clicked; riding along[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // here keeps a full-file write from erasing what the star wrote.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        .with_favorites(self.starred_tool_ids())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The rail's pinned section as tool ids, in star order — the form the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// workspace file keeps it in.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn starred_tool_ids(&self) -> Vec<String> {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.toolrail[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .favorites()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .map(|tool| tool.id().to_owned())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .collect()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Put the chrome back — the mirror of the [`Self::capture_arrangement`][m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// half that produced it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// One function because there are two callers and no way for the compiler[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// to notice when only one of them learns a new field: the startup[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// workspace and a named bookmark describe the same thing, and[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// [`ui_state::NamedArrangement`] says so in as many words. Restoring them[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// through two copies of the same eight lines is how a field comes to[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// persist but never come back from a bookmark — a bug with no compile[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// error behind it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The starred tools are the one thing it does not speak for: they live at[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the file's own level rather than inside an arrangement[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// ([`ui_state::Workspace::favorite_tools`]), precisely so that opening a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// bookmark cannot rearrange the rail the trader curated.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn restore_chrome(&mut self, chrome: &ui_state::SavedChrome) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.tz = TzOffset::new(chrome.timezone_minutes);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.dock[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .restore(chrome.dock_visible, chrome.dock_tab.map(Into::into));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.toolrail.set_dock(chrome.rail_dock.into());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.toolrail.set_visible(chrome.rail_visible);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.show_perf = chrome.perf_readings;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.progressive_history = chrome.progressive_history;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // A token this release does not know keeps the reach it had — the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // default on startup, whatever the trader picked when a bookmark is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // opened mid-session. Never a silent fallback to something else: the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // reach decides how much a press fetches.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(reach) = chrome[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .history_reach[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .as_deref()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .and_then(history_reach::HistoryReach::from_token)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.history_reach = reach;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Through the setter, so a hand-edited workspace cannot restore a span[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // the campaign could never reach.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(minutes) = chrome.history_reach_span_minutes {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.set_history_reach_span_minutes(minutes);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.venue_lead_in = chrome.venue_lead_in;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.surfaces[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .drawing_chrome[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .restore_inspector_position(chrome.inspector_position);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The tabs and the chrome as they stand — the part a startup workspace[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// and a named one describe identically, so both capture through here.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn capture_arrangement(&self) -> (Vec<ui_state::SavedTab>, ui_state::SavedChrome) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let tabs = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .tabs[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .map(|tab| ui_state::SavedTab {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                feed: tab.feed_id.clone(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                symbol: tab.symbol.clone(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                layout: tab.layout.into(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                split_fraction: Some(tab.split_fraction),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                context_collapsed: tab.context_collapsed,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                focus: Some(ui_state::SavedFocus::from_side(tab.focused_side()).0),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                focus_slot: ui_state::SavedFocus::from_side(tab.focused_side()).1,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                flow_bars: tab.flow_pane.state.spec().to_config_string(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // Only a pane that exists has an interval worth recording; a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // tab that never showed the split restores on the default,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // which is what it had.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                time_bars: tab[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .time_pane()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .map(|pane| pane.state.spec().to_config_string()),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                context_bars: tab[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .time_panes[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .map(|pane| pane.state.spec().to_config_string())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .collect(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                flow_layout: tab.flow_pane.layout.map(|layout| layout.0),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                context_layouts: tab[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .time_panes[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .map(|pane| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                        pane.layout[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                            .map_or(crate::ui_state::LAYOUT_UNRECORDED, |layout| layout.0)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    })[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .collect(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                flow_legend_collapsed: tab.flow_pane.legend_collapsed,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // A tab with no time pane has no second legend, and `false`[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // is what it will restore into when one is opened: a pane[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // that never existed cannot have been folded.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                time_legend_collapsed: tab.time_pane().is_some_and(|pane| pane.legend_collapsed),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            })[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .collect();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let chrome = ui_state::SavedChrome {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            timezone_minutes: self.tz.minutes(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            dock_visible: self.dock.visible(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            dock_tab: self.dock.tab().map(Into::into),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            rail_visible: self.toolrail.visible(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            rail_dock: self.toolrail.dock().into(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            perf_readings: self.show_perf,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Never written any more: the stars are a standing choice and live[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // at the top of the file. An arrangement that carried a copy would[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // be an arrangement that could overwrite them on open.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            legacy_favorite_tools: Vec::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            progressive_history: self.progressive_history,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The default writes no key: a workspace that says nothing about[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // the reach restores the press the button has always had, which is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // exactly what the default is.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Written whenever it differs from what the config seeds, so a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // workspace only carries an opinion its owner actually formed.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            history_reach_span_minutes: (self.history_reach_span_minutes[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                != self.config.history.reach_span_minutes)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .then_some(self.history_reach_span_minutes),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            history_reach: (self.history_reach != history_reach::HistoryReach::default())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                .then(|| self.history_reach.token().to_owned()),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            venue_lead_in: self.venue_lead_in,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            inspector_position: self.surfaces.drawing_chrome.remembered_inspector_position(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        (tabs, chrome)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Ask the operating system where to put a workspace file, off the UI[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// thread. One dialog at a time, the trades-folder picker's own pattern.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The cockpit is written to its stores *first*, so what the file gets is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the screen as it stands rather than whatever was last flushed —[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// indicator state is written debounced, and an export that raced it[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// would quietly save a cockpit the trader never had.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn open_workspace_export_picker(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if self.workspace.picker_open() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let start = crate::workspace_bundle::default_dir();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let suggested = crate::workspace_bundle::file_name_for(&self.suggested_workspace_name());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (sender, receiver) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        std::thread::Builder::new()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .name("quantick-workspace-export-picker".into())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .spawn(move || {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let mut dialog = rfd::FileDialog::new()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .set_title("Export workspace")[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .add_filter("quantick workspace", &["toml"])[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .set_file_name(suggested);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                if let Some(start) = start {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all(&start);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    dialog = dialog.set_directory(&start);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let _ = sender.send(dialog.save_file());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            })[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .expect("spawn workspace export picker thread");[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.open_picker(WorkspacePick::Export, receiver);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The same, for choosing a workspace file to open.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn open_workspace_import_picker(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if self.workspace.picker_open() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let start = crate::workspace_bundle::default_dir();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (sender, receiver) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        std::thread::Builder::new()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .name("quantick-workspace-import-picker".into())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .spawn(move || {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let mut dialog = rfd::FileDialog::new()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .set_title("Open workspace")[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .add_filter("quantick workspace", &["toml"]);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                if let Some(start) = start[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    && start.is_dir()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    dialog = dialog.set_directory(&start);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let _ = sender.send(dialog.pick_file());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            })[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .expect("spawn workspace import picker thread");[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.open_picker(WorkspacePick::Import, receiver);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Land whatever the dialog answered.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn poll_workspace_picker(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some((intent, receiver)) = self.workspace.picker() else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let choice = match receiver.try_recv() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Ok(choice) => choice,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Err(std::sync::mpsc::TryRecvError::Empty) => return,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // The dialog thread died without answering — no display server,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // a COM failure. Treated as "no answer yet" this would leave the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // field set forever and both menu entries silently dead, since[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // each refuses to open a second dialog.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Err(std::sync::mpsc::TryRecvError::Disconnected) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.workspace.close_picker();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tracing::warn!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    event_code = "WORKSPACE_PICKER_LOST",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    action = "picker_reset",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "the file dialog closed without an answer"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.note_workspace([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "The file chooser could not open — see the log. Try again.".to_owned(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let intent = *intent;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.close_picker();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // A cancelled dialog is an answer, not a failure: say nothing.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(path) = choice else { return };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        match intent {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            WorkspacePick::Export => self.export_workspace_to(&path),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            WorkspacePick::Import => self.import_workspace_from(&path),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// A starting name for an export: what the cockpit is showing, so the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// trader does not have to invent one to get a usable file name.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn suggested_workspace_name(&self) -> String {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let tab = self.active_tab();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        format!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "{} {}",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tab.symbol,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tab.flow_pane.state.spec().to_config_string()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        )[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Write every store that is still only in memory, so a bundle captured[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// next describes the screen rather than the last flush.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn flush_cockpit_stores(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The layouts file is written debounced, off the frame path. An[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // export is the one moment worth paying it immediately, or the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // bundle would carry the layouts as they stood a second ago.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.flush_layouts();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.maintain_chart_layers();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Export the whole cockpit to one file, and say what happened.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn export_workspace_to(&mut self, path: &std::path::Path) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Here rather than before the dialog: these two writes are how the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // bundle comes to describe the screen instead of the last flush, and[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // doing them up front meant a trader who pressed Cancel had still[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // silently redefined what the app opens on. It also keeps the harness[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // hook on exactly the menu's path.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.save_workspace("export");[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.flush_cockpit_stores();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let name = crate::workspace_bundle::recent_label(path);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let outcome = crate::workspace_bundle::capture([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            &name,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            crate::store_home::COCKPIT_STORES,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            &crate::workspace_bundle::live_paths,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        )[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        .and_then(|bundle| crate::workspace_bundle::write(path, &bundle).map(|()| bundle.len()));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        match outcome {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Ok(stores) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                crate::workspace_bundle::remember_recent([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    self.workspace.session_mut().recent_mut(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    path,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.refresh_recent_workspaces();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.save_workspace("export_recent");[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    event_code = "WORKSPACE_EXPORTED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    path = %path.display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    stores,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    action = "workspace_written",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "workspace exported"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.note_workspace(format!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "Workspace exported to {} — {stores} settings groups",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    path.display()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                ));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Err(error) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tracing::warn!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    event_code = "WORKSPACE_EXPORT_FAILED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    path = %path.display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    %error,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    action = "workspace_not_written",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "could not export the workspace"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.note_workspace(format!("Workspace not exported — {error}"));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Open a workspace file over the live cockpit.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Refused whole or applied whole: [`crate::workspace_bundle::apply`] checks[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// every section before writing any, so a bad file leaves the screen[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// exactly as it was and says why. What reaches the disk is then read[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// back into the running app, because a cockpit that only changed on disk[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// would be overwritten by this session's own save on exit.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn import_workspace_from(&mut self, path: &std::path::Path) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // What the debounce still holds is written first, or a bundle with no[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // layouts section would reload a file a second behind the screen.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.flush_layouts();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let outcome = crate::workspace_bundle::read(path).and_then(|bundle| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            crate::workspace_bundle::apply([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                &bundle,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                crate::store_home::COCKPIT_STORES,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                &crate::workspace_bundle::live_paths,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            )[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        match outcome {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Ok(written) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                let stores = written.len();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.reload_cockpit_stores(&written);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                crate::workspace_bundle::remember_recent([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    self.workspace.session_mut().recent_mut(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    path,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.refresh_recent_workspaces();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // The recent list lives in the workspace file the import just[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // replaced, so it has to be written back after the reload —[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                // otherwise opening a file would forget that it was opened.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.save_workspace("import_recent");[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    event_code = "WORKSPACE_IMPORTED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    path = %path.display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    stores,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    action = "cockpit_replaced",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "workspace imported"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.note_workspace(format!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "Workspace \"{}\" opened — {stores} settings groups restored",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    crate::workspace_bundle::recent_label(path)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                ));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Err(error) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                tracing::warn!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    event_code = "WORKSPACE_IMPORT_REFUSED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    path = %path.display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    %error,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    action = "cockpit_unchanged",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "workspace refused"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.note_workspace(format!("Workspace not opened — {error}"));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Read every cockpit store back into the running app.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Called after an import wrote them. Each store goes through the loader[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// it already had, so an imported cockpit is restored by exactly the code[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// that restores one at startup — a second, import-only restore path is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// how the two would drift.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn reload_cockpit_stores(&mut self, imported: &[&str]) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.added_symbols = symbols_file::load(self.workspace.symbols_path());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.drawing_presets = drawings::presets::PresetStore::load_from([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            drawings::presets::PresetStore::default_path(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.footprint_config =[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            crate::footprint_config::load(self.workspace.footprint_settings_path());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.surfaces.footprint_settings.reload_presets();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.indicator_presets =[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            preset_file::PresetStore::load(self.workspace.indicator_presets_path());[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The tab strip first, and *before* the indicators: the restore adds[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // each indicator to whatever pane is focused right now, so the tabs[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // have to be the imported ones — and the focus on the pane the file[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // describes — before a single indicator is added. Getting this order[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // wrong puts a trader's imported indicators on the tab they happened[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // to be looking at, or on its time pane.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let workspace =[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            ui_state::load(self.workspace.ui_state_path()).restore(&self.config.clone());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.restore_workspace(workspace);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.restore_chart_layers();[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The layouts come last, once the tabs are the imported ones: every[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // pane is stripped and re-seeded from the imported file.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.reload_layouts(imported);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Work out which remembered workspace files are still there.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Called when the list changes, never from the menu body — see[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// [`Self::recent_on_disk`]. The stored list keeps every entry: a file on[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// a drive that is merely unplugged today comes back when it is plugged[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// in, and only the menu is filtered.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn refresh_recent_workspaces(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let existing = crate::workspace_bundle::existing_recent(self.workspace.session().recent());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.session_mut().set_recent_on_disk(existing);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Take every indicator off every pane.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Straight off each pane's own collection rather than by walking[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// `slot_kinds`: that list is bookkeeping for the state *file*, and an[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// indicator can be on a pane without being in it — the autostart hooks[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// add without registering, and `forget_last_indicator_state_change` pops[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// an entry while leaving the indicator on screen. Clearing the list[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// would have left those behind for the imported set to stack on top of.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn clear_indicators(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        /// Empty one pane, view and worker alike.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        fn strip(pane: &mut crate::pane::ChartPane) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let slots: Vec<SlotId> = pane.indicators.all().iter().map(|view| view.slot).collect();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            for slot in slots {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.indicators.remove(slot);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                pane.indicator_worker.send(IndicatorCommand::Remove(slot));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for tab in &mut self.tabs {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Every pane the tab holds, not the two it used to. `panes_mut`[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // rather than `pane_mut(Time)`: the latter falls back to the flow[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // pane when a tab was never split, which would strip it twice, and[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // it stops at the *first* context chart — so the second stacked[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // chart kept its indicators while `slot_kinds` was cleared out from[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // under them, and the imported set stacked on top.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            for pane in tab.panes_mut() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                strip(pane);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.slot_kinds.clear();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.operator_slots.clear();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.script_files.clear();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.pending_hidden.clear();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.pending_styles.clear();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.mark_indicator_state_dirty();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Show the trader where the cockpit is kept, and open it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The answer to "where does this thing save my setup?" — which, before[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the durable home, had no single answer at all.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn reveal_cockpit_home(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(home) = crate::store_home::home() else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.note_workspace([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "This system reports no documents folder — quantick keeps the cockpit beside \[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                 wherever it was launched from"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .to_owned(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_workspace(format!("Cockpit saved in {}", home.display()));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The journal panel's opener, not a second copy of the platform[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // table: two of them means the next fix lands on one and misses the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // other. Best effort — the path is on the status line either way, so[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // a system with no file manager still answers the question.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        crate::paper_trading::reveal_folder(&home);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Write the workspace and say so on the status bar.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The notice is the point of the explicit action: a trader who arranges a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// cockpit and clicks Save wants to know it is kept, and "it looks the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// same" is not an answer. A failed write says *that* instead — being told[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// "saved" and finding out at the next launch is the one outcome worth[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// engineering against.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn save_workspace(&mut self, reason: &'static str) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let workspace = self.capture_workspace();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let saved = ui_state::save(self.workspace.ui_state_path(), &workspace);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.session_mut().note_write(saved);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "UI_STATE_SAVED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            path = %self.workspace.ui_state_path().display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tabs = workspace.tabs.len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            saved,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            reason,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = if saved { "workspace_written" } else { "workspace_not_written" },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "workspace save"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_workspace(if saved {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            format!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "Workspace saved — quantick opens on {} {}",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                workspace.tabs.len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                if workspace.tabs.len() == 1 {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "chart tab"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "chart tabs"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            )[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "Workspace could not be saved — see the log".to_owned()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Update *only* where the properties popup goes, in the workspace file as[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// it already stands. Says whether anything was written.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Deliberately not [`Self::save_workspace`]. That one captures the whole[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// window — every tab, its market, its bar rule, the layout, the window[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// size — and adopts it as the startup screen. Parking a popup is not a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// statement about any of that: a trader who opens six tabs to research[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// something, then nudges the popup out of the way, must not find those six[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// tabs waiting for them tomorrow. The switch that governs whole-window[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// saves says "when the window closes", and this write happens mid-session,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// so it has no business speaking for the tabs.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// A file with no chrome section is left alone rather than created: there[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// is nothing to update, and inventing a startup workspace out of a drag[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// would undo a `Reset startup layout` the trader just asked for. The exit[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// save is what creates the file, and it carries the position with it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// No toast either. The "Workspace saved" line answers a deliberate *Save*,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// and repeating it after every small gesture would turn the window's one[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// acknowledgement channel into wallpaper — but the log still names the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// write, so a position that went missing is answerable.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn write_inspector_position(&mut self) -> bool {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let mut file = ui_state::load(self.workspace.ui_state_path());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(chrome) = file.chrome.as_mut() else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return false;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let position = self.surfaces.drawing_chrome.remembered_inspector_position();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if chrome.inspector_position == position {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return false;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        chrome.inspector_position = position;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let saved = ui_state::save(self.workspace.ui_state_path(), &file);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "UI_STATE_POPUP_POSITION_SAVED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            path = %self.workspace.ui_state_path().display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            parked = position.is_some(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            saved,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = if saved { "position_written" } else { "position_not_written" },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "properties popup position"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        saved[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Write the bookmarks without disturbing the startup arrangement.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Reads the file back and swaps only the named entries, rather than[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// capturing the live window: saving a bookmark must not redefine what the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// app opens on, and `capture_workspace` describes the screen *now*, which[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// is exactly what the startup arrangement must not become.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn write_bookmarks(&mut self) -> bool {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let bookmarks = self.workspace.session().bookmarks().to_vec();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let written = self.edit_workspace_file("UI_STATE_BOOKMARKS_WRITTEN", |file| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            file.saved = bookmarks;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.session_mut().note_write(written);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        written[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Change one standing choice in the workspace file, leaving everything[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// else in it exactly as it was. `true` when the change reached the disk.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// This file holds three choices that are not descriptions of the screen —[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the named bookmarks, the replay folder, the starred tools. Each is made[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// by a single click and each is written on the spot rather than at exit,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// because "it forgot again" must not be one crash away. Each used to[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// hand-roll the same read-swap-write, and three copies were three chances[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// to differ: two carried `save_on_exit` through and one did not, so a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// trader with autosave off who picked a replay folder before the file[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// existed had autosave quietly switched back on at the next launch.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// `save_on_exit` rides along here because it is the one live setting that[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// belongs to the *file* rather than to any arrangement inside it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// A file this build cannot read is never rewritten — see[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// [`ui_state::load_for_edit`]. `workspace_saved` is deliberately not[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// touched: whether a startup *arrangement* exists is a different question[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// from whether this file does, and the caller answers it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    fn edit_workspace_file([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        &mut self,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        event_code: &'static str,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        edit: impl FnOnce(&mut ui_state::Workspace),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ) -> bool {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(mut file) = ui_state::load_for_edit(self.workspace.ui_state_path()) else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.note_workspace([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "The workspace file could not be read, so it was left alone — see the log"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .to_owned(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return false;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        file.save_on_exit = self.workspace.session().save_on_exit();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        edit(&mut file);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let written = ui_state::save(self.workspace.ui_state_path(), &file);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            path = %self.workspace.ui_state_path().display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            written,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = if written { "file_updated" } else { "file_not_written" },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "a standing choice was written to the workspace file"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        written[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Write down the replay folder the trader just pointed the browser at,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// without disturbing anything else the workspace file holds.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The same read-swap-write as [`Self::write_bookmarks`], and for the same[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// reason: this is a standing choice, not a description of the screen, so[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// it must not wait for a clean exit and must not drag the current[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// arrangement into the file with it.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn write_replay_folder(&mut self, folder: Option<&str>) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let stored = folder.map(str::to_owned);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let written = self.edit_workspace_file("REPLAY_FOLDER_REMEMBERED", |file| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            file.replay_folder = stored;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.session_mut().note_write(written);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "REPLAY_FOLDER_REMEMBERED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            folder = folder.unwrap_or_default(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            written,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = if folder.is_some() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "store_replay_folder"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "forget_replay_folder"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "the replay folder is now the one this workspace opens on"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Write down the *day before* choice the trader just made, without[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// disturbing anything else the workspace file holds.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The same read-swap-write as [`Self::write_replay_folder`], and for the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// same reason: whether yesterday is on the chart is a standing choice,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// not a description of the screen, so it must not wait for a clean exit.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn write_replay_day_before(&mut self, enabled: bool) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let written = self.edit_workspace_file("REPLAY_DAY_BEFORE_REMEMBERED", |file| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            file.replay_day_before = Some(enabled);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace.session_mut().note_write(written);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "REPLAY_DAY_BEFORE_REMEMBERED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            enabled,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            written,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = if enabled {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "join_day_before"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "chosen_day_only"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "the day before is now what this workspace opens recordings with"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Write down the tools the trader just starred or unstarred, without[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// disturbing anything else the workspace holds.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The same read-swap-write as [`Self::write_replay_folder`], through the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// same [`Self::edit_workspace_file`], with two things of its own.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// First, `save_on_exit` does not gate it. That switch governs whether[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// closing the window redefines the *arrangement* — which tabs open, how[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the panes are split. A starred tool is not an arrangement, and a trader[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// who turned autosave off to stop their layout drifting has not asked to[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// rebuild their rail every session.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Second, `workspace_saved` is left alone. It answers "is there a startup[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// arrangement to reset?", and starring a tool does not create one — a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// fresh install whose only saved thing is a star would otherwise light up[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// a Reset entry that promises to forget a layout nobody ever saved.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn write_favorites(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // A run under `QUANTICK_TOOL_FAVORITES` is wearing a rail the harness[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // dressed it in, not one the trader curated, and the same guard the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // replay folder gets applies: a validation run must not write a QA[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // list into the trader's workspace. The hook stages a screen; it does[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // not make choices on their behalf.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if self.workspace.session().favorites_are_staged() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                event_code = "TOOL_FAVORITES_NOT_WRITTEN",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                action = "staged_by_hook",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "a run under QUANTICK_TOOL_FAVORITES does not write the rail down"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let tools = self.starred_tool_ids();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let count = tools.len();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let written = self.edit_workspace_file("TOOL_FAVORITES_REMEMBERED", |file| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            file.favorite_tools = tools;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "TOOL_FAVORITES_REMEMBERED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tools = count,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            written,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = if written { "favorites_written" } else { "favorites_not_written" },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "the rail's pinned tools are now what this workspace opens on"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Keep the window as it stands under `name`.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// A bookmark, not a startup setting: what the app opens on is untouched.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The reason to name an arrangement is usually to have somewhere to come[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// back *to*, and a "save this so I can return to it" that also redefined[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the opening screen would be the opposite of a safety net.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// An existing name is replaced rather than duplicated — that is what[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// "save as" means everywhere else, and it spares the menu a list of five[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// entries called "scalp".[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn save_named_workspace(&mut self, name: &str) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(name) = ui_state::clean_workspace_name(name) else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.note_workspace("A workspace needs a name".to_owned());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (tabs, chrome) = self.capture_arrangement();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let entry = ui_state::NamedArrangement {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            name: name.clone(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            window: self.window_size,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            active_tab: self.active_tab,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tabs,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            chrome: Some(chrome),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let replaced = match self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .workspace[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .session_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .bookmarks_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .iter_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .find(|held| held.name == name)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            Some(held) => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                *held = entry;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                true[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            None => {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.workspace.session_mut().bookmarks_mut().push(entry);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                false[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let written = self.write_bookmarks();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "UI_STATE_NAMED_SAVED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            name = %name,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            replaced,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            saved = self.workspace.session().bookmarks().len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            written,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = if written { "bookmark_written" } else { "bookmark_not_written" },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "named workspace saved"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_workspace(if written {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let verb = if replaced { "replaced" } else { "saved" };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            format!("Workspace \"{name}\" {verb} — reopen it from Workspace → Open")[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            format!("\"{name}\" could not be saved — see the log")[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Put the window back the way the bookmark called `name` recorded it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The saved markets are opened as new tabs and the tabs that were on[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// screen are closed afterwards, rather than the reverse: `close_tab`[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// refuses to close the last tab — a window with no market has nothing to[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// draw — so growing before shrinking is what lets the whole strip be[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// replaced. Closing goes through the same path a `Ctrl+W` takes, so a[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// simulated position ends in the labeled, journaled flatten the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// paper-trading contract promises instead of vanishing with its tab.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The startup workspace is left alone. Opening a bookmark is a thing you[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// do to *this session*; making it the opening screen is `Save workspace`,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// one entry above.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn open_named_workspace(&mut self, name: &str) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let Some(entry) = self[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .workspace[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .session()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .bookmarks()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .iter()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .find(|held| held.name == name)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .cloned()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.note_workspace(format!("No workspace called \"{name}\""));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if entry.tabs.is_empty() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // `restore` drops empty bookmarks at load, so this is only[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // reachable from a file edited under a running app.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.note_workspace(format!("\"{name}\" has no market left to open"));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let replaced = self.tabs.len();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for saved in &entry.tabs {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.open_tab([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                saved.feed.clone(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                saved.symbol.clone(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                BarSpec::parse(&saved.flow_bars).ok(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let context_intervals =[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                saved_context_intervals(&saved.context_bars, saved.time_bars.as_deref());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let opened = self.tabs.len() - 1;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.tabs[opened].restore_canvas([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                CanvasLayout::from(saved.layout),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                saved.split_fraction,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                saved.context_collapsed,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                saved.focus.map(|focus| focus.to_side(saved.focus_slot)),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                &context_intervals,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                LegendFold {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    flow: saved.flow_legend_collapsed,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    time: saved.time_legend_collapsed,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.tabs[opened].set_opening_layouts(saved.flow_layout, &saved.context_layouts);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        for _ in 0..replaced {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.close_tab(0);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The pinned section is deliberately untouched: the stars live beside[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // the chrome rather than inside it, and [`Self::restore_chrome`] does[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // not speak for them. A bookmark rearranges the cockpit; the tools the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // trader keeps at hand are not part of the arrangement, and a bookmark[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // named before they starred anything used to wipe the rail on open.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(chrome) = &entry.chrome {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.restore_chrome(chrome);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.active_tab = entry.active_tab.min(self.tabs.len().saturating_sub(1));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let config = self.config.clone();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.active_tab_mut().refresh_chip_label(&config);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "UI_STATE_NAMED_OPENED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            name = %name,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            tabs = self.tabs.len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            closed = replaced,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            active = self.active_tab,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = "replace_tab_strip",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "named workspace opened"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_workspace(format!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "Opened \"{name}\" — {} {}",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.tabs.len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            if self.tabs.len() == 1 {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "chart tab"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "chart tabs"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        ));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Forget the bookmark called `name`. The window on screen is untouched —[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// deleting a bookmark throws away a way back, not the place you are.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn delete_named_workspace(&mut self, name: &str) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let before = self.workspace.session().bookmarks().len();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .session_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .bookmarks_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .retain(|held| held.name != name);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if self.workspace.session().bookmarks().len() == before {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let written = self.write_bookmarks();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "UI_STATE_NAMED_DELETED",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            name = %name,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            remaining = self.workspace.session().bookmarks().len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            written,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = if written { "bookmark_forgotten" } else { "file_not_written" },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "named workspace deleted"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_workspace(if written {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            format!("Workspace \"{name}\" deleted")[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            format!("\"{name}\" could not be deleted — see the log")[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Forget the saved workspace: the next launch opens on the configured[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// defaults. The window on screen is deliberately left alone — a trader[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// resetting their *startup* layout mid-session has not asked to have the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// charts they are reading rearranged under them.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn forget_workspace(&mut self) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Reset clears the *startup* arrangement. The bookmarks survive it,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // because coming back after a reset is the whole reason to name one:[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // deleting the safety net as part of the act it exists to undo would[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // be the single worst thing this menu could do.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let bookmarks_kept = !self.workspace.session().bookmarks().is_empty();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The starred tools survive it too, and for a plainer reason: they[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // were never part of the arrangement being reset. Resetting a layout[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // is not asking to rebuild the rail by hand — and the same goes for[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // every other standing choice this file holds. The replay folder and[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // the Open-recent list are facts about this installation; the entry[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // resets a *layout* and must not quietly take them with it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let stars = self.starred_tool_ids();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let kept = bookmarks_kept[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            || !stars.is_empty()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            || !self.workspace.session().recent().is_empty()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            || self.replay_view.stored_pick().is_some();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let bookmarks = self.workspace.session().bookmarks().to_vec();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let stars_kept = !stars.is_empty();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let forgotten = if kept {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // Edited rather than rebuilt from the defaults: writing a fresh[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // `Workspace` would carry only what this function remembered to[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // thread through it, and the fields it forgot would be reset by[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // omission. Clearing the arrangement names what goes; everything[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            // unnamed stays by construction.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.edit_workspace_file("UI_STATE_FORGOTTEN", |file| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                file.tabs.clear();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                file.chrome = None;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                file.window = None;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                file.active_tab = 0;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                file.saved = bookmarks;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                file.favorite_tools = stars;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            })[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            ui_state::forget(self.workspace.ui_state_path())[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The file still exists while it holds standing choices, so Reset[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // stays available — it is now a no-op for the startup screen and the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // entry says as much. A reset that *failed* leaves the old[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // arrangement on disk and so leaves the entry live: the trader has to[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // be able to try again, and telling them "nothing saved yet" while the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // next launch still reopens the layout they discarded would be a lie.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.workspace[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .session_mut()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            .set_saved(if forgotten { kept } else { true });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "UI_STATE_FORGOTTEN",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            path = %self.workspace.ui_state_path().display(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            forgotten,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            bookmarks_kept = self.workspace.session().bookmarks().len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            favorites_kept = self.toolrail.favorites().len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = if forgotten { "open_on_config_defaults" } else { "workspace_kept" },[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "workspace reset"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // What survived is named, never left to be discovered. A trader who[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // resets a layout and is told nothing else assumes nothing else was[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // kept — and would go looking for stars that are still there.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let survivors = match (bookmarks_kept, stars_kept) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            (true, true) => format!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                " {} saved {} and the starred tools kept.",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.workspace.session().bookmarks().len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                if self.workspace.session().bookmarks().len() == 1 {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "workspace"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "workspaces"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            ),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            (true, false) => format!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                " {} saved {} kept.",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                self.workspace.session().bookmarks().len(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                if self.workspace.session().bookmarks().len() == 1 {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "workspace"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    "workspaces"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            ),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            (false, true) => " The starred tools are kept.".to_owned(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            (false, false) => String::new(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        };[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.note_workspace(if forgotten {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            format!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                "Startup layout reset — the next launch opens on the configured default.{survivors}"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            )[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        } else {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "Workspace could not be reset — see the log".to_owned()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Hand the app the window it is drawing into.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Called once from `main`, which is where eframe offers the handle: the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// hook that needs it (`raw_input_hook`) is given only the input. One[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// registration line rather than a constructor argument, because every[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// other construction path — every test — wants the `None` this defaults[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// to, which is also what every non-Windows target gets.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    pub fn attach_surface(&mut self, handle: &impl raw_window_handle::HasWindowHandle) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.surface = window_scale::SurfaceProbe::new(handle);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Take the window manager's own maximise, once, on the first frame.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Through [`egui::ViewportCommand::Maximized`] rather than the viewport[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// builder's `with_maximized`: eframe 0.29 does not honour that flag beside[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// an `inner_size`, and a hook that silently opens a 1100×650 window while[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// reporting success is worse than no hook — a validation run would[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// photograph the wrong state and call it a pass. The command is the one[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the platform runs when a hand hits the title bar, which is the state[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// this hook exists to reach.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn apply_maximize_hook(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if !self.harness.take_maximize() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            return;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        ctx.send_viewport_cmd(egui::ViewportCommand::Maximized(true));[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        tracing::info!([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            target: "quantick::app",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            schema_version = 1_u8,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            event_code = "WINDOW_MAXIMIZE_AUTOSTART",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            action = "maximize",[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            "QUANTICK_WINDOW_MAXIMIZED asked for the maximised layout"[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        );[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Keep the window size the workspace would record, flush a popup the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// trader just re-parked, and take the exit save when the window is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// closing.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// **Per-frame cost**: two reads off the frame's own input state, a float[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// compare and a `bool` test. No save is on this path — each of the three[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// happens on one frame: the frame a drag ends, and the frame the close is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// requested, when the window is going away anyway.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// The size is tracked here rather than read at exit because by then the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// viewport has already been asked to close: what a workspace should[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// remember is the window the trader was working in, not whatever the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// platform reports on the way out.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn maintain_workspace(&mut self, ctx: &egui::Context) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        let (size, closing) = ctx.input(|input| {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            let viewport = input.viewport();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            ([m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                viewport[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .inner_rect[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                    .map(|rect| [rect.width(), rect.height()]),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m                viewport.close_requested(),[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            )[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        });[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if let Some(size) = size[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            && size[0] > 0.0[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            && size[1] > 0.0[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.window_size = Some(size);[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Where the trader parks the properties popup is kept the moment the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // hand comes off it, without a trip through the Workspace menu: the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // window they dragged out of the way is the window they expect back,[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // and a memory that only survived a clean exit would lose it to the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // one session that ended badly. Only that one field is written — see[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // [`Self::write_inspector_position`] for why a drag must not adopt the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // tab strip as a startup screen.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        //[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // Gated on the same switch the exit save is, because the menu already[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // promises it: "Off, only Save workspace changes what quantick opens[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // on." A trader who curates their startup layout by hand still gets the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // position within the session — it is live state — and an explicit Save[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // still records it.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        //[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // The closing frame takes the exit save instead: it writes the whole[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // window, this position included, so running both would serialise the[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        // same file twice on the way out.[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        if closing && self.workspace.session().save_on_exit() {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.inspector_position_dirty = false;[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.save_workspace("exit");[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        } else if std::mem::take(&mut self.inspector_position_dirty)[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            && self.workspace.session().save_on_exit()[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m            self.write_inspector_position();[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        }[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[1;36m+[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// Post a Workspace-menu answer through the window's one acknowledgement[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// channel ([`Toast`]).[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    ///[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// No Undo: the file it replaced is gone, and `Reset startup layout` is[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    /// the honest way back rather than a button that pretends otherwise.[m
[32m+[m[32m    pub(super) fn note_workspace(&mut self, message: String) {[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m        self.surfaces.toast.note(message, Instant::now());[m
[1;36m+[m[1;36m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
